[EVENT: ISR14467]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Zomet Gome
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Globes; Haaretz [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, about 2,000 protested at Zomet Gome (Zefat, HaZafon) against the Netanyahu-led coalition's judicial overhaul plan. Activists took to the streets around the country following Prime Minister Netanyahu's recent announcement on a temporary freeze to the legislation, signaling that protests will continue even as negotiations for a compromised reform plan take place.

[EVENT: ISR14445]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Ashdod
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Globes; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, a protest took place in Ashdod (Ashqelon, HaDarom) against the Netanyahu-led coalition's judicial overhaul plan. A National Unity MK spoke at the protest, in addition to former Public Security Minister and Labour Party MK Omer Bar-Lev. Dr. Eliad Shraga of the Movement for Quality Government in Israel also spoke. Activists took to the streets around the country following Prime Minister Netanyahu's recent announcement on a temporary freeze to the legislation, signaling that protests will continue even as negotiations for a compromised reform plan take place.

---

[EVENT: ISR14453]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Kfar Saba
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Actor2: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Im Tirtzu; Jerusalem Post; News 0404; Tzomet HaSharon [Other-Subnational]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, about 21,000 protested in Kfar Saba (Petah Tiqwa, HaMerkaz) against the Netanyahu-led coalition's judicial overhaul plan. Among the speakers at the protest were former Yisrael Beytenu MK Sharon Roffe Ofir and Meretz political activist Uri Saki. Hundreds of right-wing activists, including many from Im Tirtzu, held a counter-protest in favor of the judicial reforms. Activists took to the streets around the country following Prime Minister Netanyahu's recent announcement on a temporary freeze to the legislation, signaling that protests will continue even as negotiations for a compromised reform plan take place.

---

[EVENT: ISR14429]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Protests > Excessive force against protesters | Disorder: Political violence; Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Calcalist; Globes; Haaretz; Kan News; Walla!News [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, police forces used 'disproportionate force' against a protester who was leaving Ayalon Highway in Tel Aviv city during protests against the judicial reform plan. According to reports, police forces whipped and injured a female protester as she was leaving the highway.

[EVENT: ISR14462]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Haifa
Type: Protests > Protest with intervention | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Calcalist; Globes; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Kan News; Walla!News [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, about 20,000 protested in Haifa city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's judicial overhaul plan. TV host and comedian Lior Schlein was among the speakers at the protest. University faculty were present. Female activists dressed as 'handmaids' from Building an Alternative also took part in the protest. Police forces arrested one protester holding a Palestinian flag for 'violating public order' and allegedly trying to push his way through police forces to get to the protest. Police forces were blocking anti-occupation protesters holding the Palestinian flags from reaching the central protest. Activists took to the streets around the country following Prime Minister Netanyahu's recent announcement on a temporary freeze to the legislation, signaling that protests will continue even as negotiations for a compromised reform plan take place.

---

[EVENT: ISR14444]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Actor2: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Black Flag Movement; Calcalist; Globes; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Kan News; Walla!News [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, about 200,000 protested in Tel Aviv city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's judicial overhaul plan. Among the speakers at the protest were Black Flag Movement founder and Professor Nathan Netanyahu, who is the prime minister's cousin. Yair Lapid and Merav Michaeli were present at the protest, in addition to Prime Minister Netanyahu's former Office Director-General. Students, teachers, army reservists, and lawyers took part in the protest. About 500 Likud supporters held a counter-protest. Activists took to the streets around the country following Prime Minister Netanyahu's recent announcement on a temporary freeze to the legislation, signaling that protests will continue even as negotiations for a compromised reform plan take place.

[EVENT: ISR14511]
Date: 2023-04-01
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Riots > Violent demonstration | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Rioters (Israel) [Rioters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Calcalist; Globes; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Kan News; Walla!News [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 1 April 2023, hundreds blocked traffic and demonstrated on Ayalon Highway in Tel Aviv city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's judicial overhaul plan. Police forces arrested 19 total rioters, including both anti- and pro-judicial reform demonstrators. Three right-wing demonstrators were arrested while carrying pepper spray, a knife, and a taser. Rioters clashed with police forces, who used water cannons, mounted police, and a 'sound cannon' to disperse demonstrators who were blocking traffic on the highway. A group of pro-reform rioters attacked anti-reform demonstrators and stole a pride flag from them. Activists took to the streets around the country following Prime Minister Netanyahu's recent announcement on a temporary freeze to the legislation, signaling that demonstrations will continue even as negotiations for a compromised reform plan take place.

---

[EVENT: ISR14431]

Sources: Arab 48; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:

A female protester was injured and taken to hospital.

[EVENT: ISR14508]
Date: 2023-04-06
Country: Israel | Location: Shlomi
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Lebanon) [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Israel) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Jerusalem Post; N12; Times of Israel; Walla!News [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 6 April 2023, Palestinian factions within Lebanon fired 34 rockets toward localities in northern Israel, causing sirens to sound in Hanita and Shlomi (Akko, HaZafon), among other locations. The iron dome intercepted 25 of the rockets, while five landed within Israeli territory, presumably near Shlomi, and four were unaccounted for. At least three Israelis were lightly injured from shrapnel and another person was injured while running to the bomb shelter. Also, property damaged was caused and a number of fires broke out as a result of the rockets. Initial Israeli security assessments indicate that Hamas operatives in Lebanon fired the rockets.

[EVENT: ISR14709]
Date: 2023-05-02
Country: Israel | Location: Sderot
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PIJ: Palestinian Islamic Jihad [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Israel) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Arutz Sheva; Israeli Defense Forces; Jerusalem Post; Ma'ariv; N12; Times of Israel; Walla!News; Ynet [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 2 May 2023, Palestinian armed groups fired rockets toward localities in southern Israel, causing sirens to sound in Niram, Erez, Ibbim, Sderot (Ashqelon, HaDarom), and other nearby towns. Between 2 to 3 May, 104 rockets were fired from Gaza toward southern Israel. At least two direct hits were reported in Sderot on 2 May, one hitting a factory and one hitting a residential area, causing damage to a vehicle. As a result of the direct hit on the factory, three workers, including a Chinese national, were injured. The Chinese worker was injured moderately and the other two were lightly injured. About nine other people required hospitalization, either due to anxiety attacks or injuries sustained while running to bomb shelters. Both the PIJ and Hamas claimed responsibility for the rocket fire, stating that it was in response to the death of a hunger-striking PIJ prisoner.

---

[EVENT: ISR14708]
Date: 2023-05-02
Country: Israel | Location: Sderot
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PIJ: Palestinian Islamic Jihad [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Israel) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Arutz Sheva; Israeli Defense Forces; Jerusalem Post; Ma'ariv; N12; Times of Israel; Walla!News; Ynet [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 2 May 2023, Palestinian armed groups fired rockets toward localities in southern Israel, causing sirens to sound in Niram, Erez, Ibbim, Sderot (Ashqelon, HaDarom), and other nearby towns. Between 2 to 3 May, 104 rockets were fired from Gaza toward southern Israel. The iron dome intercepted 24 rockets, while 11 landed in the sea, 14 landed within Gaza, and 48 landed in open areas, many in the area of Sderot. The final seven rockets were unaccounted for at the time of reporting. About 12 people required hospitalization due to the rocket fire, from direct hits, injuries sustained while running to bomb shelters, or anxiety attacks. Both the PIJ and Hamas claimed responsibility for the rocket fire, stating that it was in response to the death of a hunger-striking PIJ prisoner.

This included a protest from the 'Mothers on the Frontlines' organization outside of the IDF's Induction Center in the Tel Hashomer neighborhood of Ramat Gan. This protest was part of a nationwide 'Day of Equality,' during which activists argued that social equality would be harmed by the proposed judicial reforms.

[EVENT: ISR14725]
Date: 2023-05-04
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Actor2: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Black Flag Movement; Calcalist; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Times of Israel; Walla!News [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 4 May 2023, thousands of activists protested in several locations in Tel Aviv city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's proposed judicial overhaul legislation. Hundreds blocked major roads and intersections throughout the day, including Ayalon Highway and Kaplan Street. Police forces arrested at least three for disorderly conduct. On Kaplan Street, a cab driver attacked protesters. In response, protesters broke the windshield of his cab. About 100 held a protest outside of Likud MK Gila Gamliel's house. About 1,500 activists, including many from the LGBTQ+ community, held an 'equality march' protested against the increasing power of religious courts. Hundreds of female activists from Building an Alternative, many of whom dressed as 'handmaids,' protested outside of the city's Chief Rabbinate office. Students, professors, high-tech workers, and Black Flag Movement activists also took part in the day's protests. These protests were part of a nationwide 'Day of Equality,' during which activists argued that social equality would be harmed by the proposed judicial reforms. Hundreds of pro and anti-judicial overhaul activists protested simultaneously outside of former Surpeme Court president Aharon Barak's house, with Im Tirtzu activists among the pro-overhaul camp and army reservist in the anti-overhaul protest in support of Barak.

---

[EVENT: ISR14690]

Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0

DESCRIPTION:
On 6 May 2023, about 110,000 to 180,000 protested in Tel Aviv city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's proposed judicial overhaul legislation. Former Justice Minister and Zionist Union MK Tzipi Livni spoke at the protest, in addition to a Druze Israeli activist and founder of the Black Flag Movement Shikma Bressler. Students, high-tech workers, army veterans from the 1973 Yom Kippur War, and LGBTQ activists took part in the protest. An opposition lawmaker from the Poland also participated in the protest. Israeli Arab and Jewish activists took part in the anti-overhaul demonstration, while also protesting police inaction in addressing rising violence in the Arab community. Activists also protested rising violence against women. People threw eggs and a bottle at several protesters.

[EVENT: ISR14869]
Date: 2023-05-13
Country: Israel | Location: Jerusalem
Type: Strategic developments > Agreement | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Actor2: PIJ: Palestinian Islamic Jihad [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Times of Israel; Ynet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 13 May 2023, the Government of Israel and the PIJ reached a ceasefire agreement following four days of fighting. Since 10 May, the PIJ fired about 1,469 projectiles toward Israel. About 290 fell within Gaza, while roughly 439 were intercepted and most of the others fell in open areas. Several rockets landed in populated areas, resulting in two deaths, about 27 physical injuries, and about 42 more hospitalizations due to anxiety attacks. Over the same time period, Israeli military forces carried out hundreds of strikes in Gaza, including targeted assassinations of high ranking PIJ officials. Coded at Jerusalem (Judean Mountains).

Fatalities: 0
Sources: Globes; Haaretz; Israel Hayom; Jerusalem Post; Times of Israel; Walla!News; Ynet [National]

DESCRIPTION:

Some protesters also demonstrated against ongoing Israeli military operations in the Gaza Strip. Protesters blocked traffic, presumably by standing or walking on the roads, and police forces dispersed them without using force. Standing Together and Crime Minister were among the organizations that took part in the protest. Due to the ongoing rocket fire from Gaza, turnout at protests around the country was lower than usual.

Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Shelling/artillery/missile attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PIJ: Palestinian Islamic Jihad [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Palestine) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Arutz Sheva; Globes; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Times of Israel [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 13 May 2023, the PIJ fired rockets from Gaza toward localities in southern Israel. A direct hit was reported at a work site in Shoqeda (Beer Sheva, HaDarom), where one Palestinian from Gaza who was working at the site was killed. Another Palestinian and an Israeli Bedouin were injured. On 13 May, the PIJ fired at least 135 rockets toward Israel, bringing the total to about 1,469 projectiles since 10 May.

[EVENT: ISR15078]
Date: 2023-06-03
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Black Flag Movement; Calcalist; Globes; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; N12; Times of Israel; Walla!News [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 June 2023, about 95,000 to 155,000 protested in Tel Aviv city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's proposed judicial overhaul legislation. Among the speakers at the protest were a former Likud MK, a former IDF brigadier general, and a protest leader who was was arrested the previous day during a protest in Caesarea. Army reservists, female activists from Building an Alternative, LGTBQ activists, Black Flag Movement activists, anti-occupation activists from Breaking the Silence and Hadash, Israeli Arab and Jewish activists from Standing Together, as well as workers from the high-tech industry, whose sector they believe will be particularly affected, all took part in the protest.

[EVENT: ISR15013]
Date: 2023-06-03
Country: Israel | Location: Har Harif
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Egypt (2014-) [External/Other forces]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 4
Sources: Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Kan News; Walla!News; Ynet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 June 2023, an Egyptian policeman killed three Israeli soldiers after crossing over the border near Har Harif (Beer Sheva, HaDarom), before he was shot dead in a firefight with Israeli military forces. According to reports, the Egyptian policeman crossed the border into Israel and killed two Israeli soldiers who were on guard duty. A few hours later, Israeli military forces found the assailant and got into a shootout. During the armed clash, an additional Israeli soldier was killed, another soldier was lightly injured, and the assailant was shot dead. Egyptian and Israeli authorities are cooperating with each other on jointly investigating the incident, and it's not believed that it will negatively impact Israel-Egypt relations.

---

Country: Israel | Location: Herzliya
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Black Flag Movement [Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 22 June 2023, Black Flag Movement activists protested outside of the house of the owner of Channel 14 News in Herzliya (Tel Aviv), presumably because they believe he is spreading lies and propaganda about anti-reform protesters through the news outlet.

---

[EVENT: ISR15231]
Date: 2023-06-22
Country: Israel | Location: Jerusalem
Type: Riots > Violent demonstration | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Rioters (Israel) [Rioters]
Actor2: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Arutz Sheva; Times of Israel; Ynet [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 22 June 2023, dozens of far-right Lehava activists demonstrated outside of a convention of Messianic Jews in Jerusalem (Judean Mountains). Demonstrators attempted to break into the convention center, tried to block people from getting into the event, and clashed with Israeli police forces. One demonstrator was arrested for allegedly attacking an officer.

---

[EVENT: ISR15261]
Date: 2023-06-22
Country: Israel | Location: Tel Aviv
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Israel) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Black Flag Movement [Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 22 June 2023, Black Flag Movement activists protested outside of the house of Likud MK and Minister of Intelligence Gila Gamliel in Tel Aviv city against the Netanyahu-led coalition's proposed judicial overhaul legislation.
